Also trigger when the user asks to write a commit message or wants to understand how to format one. ([TEHIK-EE/ai-generic-skills](https://github.com/TEHIK-EE/ai-generic-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/tehik-dev-skills/skills/conventional-commit))
- **conventional-commit-message**: Generate the final Conventional Commit message text by inspecting the intended git commit surface, with staged changes as the default source of truth. Use this whenever the user asks for a commit message, wants staged changes or the working tree summarized into a Conventional Commit, asks what type or scope to use, or needs help turning staged changes into release-note-ready commit text instead of code-level narration. ([meza/skills](https://github.com/meza/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/conventional-commit-message/skills/conventional-commit-message) · ★1)
- **conventional-commit-message**: Generate high-quality Conventional Commit messages for repositories using conventional-changelog-conventionalcommits. ([conventional-changelog/conventional-changelog](https://github.com/conventional-changelog/conventional-changelog) · 595 installs)
- **conventional-commit-message**: Generate a conventional commit message from files edited in this conversation and current git state. Use when the user asks for a commit message, wants to commit changes, or says "what should my commit say". ([pietrbr/claude-plugins](https://github.com/pietrbr/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/conventional-commit-message/skills/conventional-commit-message))
